Sigil server dependency notices

Resolved runtime and build dependency versions are pinned in Cargo.lock.
Project source licensing remains unselected. Upstream notices follow.
HTTP/TLS provider-egress notices are additionally in Client-ThirdParty.txt.

Local patch notice: ece-native 0.5.0 is pinned to vendor/ece-native, based on
upstream commit 6b0a3f10377af3c9401a7c1a60b1e34d41155c5d with the MIT option.
Record-size, missing-record and padding-delimiter validation were corrected.
Upstream cipher/KDF code remains unchanged. See vendor/ece-native/README.md.

IETF RFC 9421, Appendix B.1.4 and B.2.6 test-vector data
https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9421.html
Authors: Annabelle Backman, Justin Richer, Manu Sporny.
The Ed25519 test key, signature base and expected signature in
server/src/federation_auth.rs are derived from these published IETF examples.
No general-purpose signature implementation was copied from the RFC.
